40. Beyond Binary: Sim-to-Real Dexterous Manipulation with Physics-Grounded Contact Representation

  • 链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28812v1
  • 来源：arxiv
  • 摘要：A primary bottleneck in contact-rich manipulation is the difficulty of collecting real-world data. Sim-to-real reinforcement learning offers a scalable alternative, but the simulation-reality gap prevents information-dense modalities like touch from being effectively used. Existing sim-to-real methods often mitigate this gap by simplifying tactile data into coarse low-dimensional features -- sacrificing the richness required for complex manipulation. In this work, we introduce Center-of-Pressure (CoP), an effective tactile representation grounded in physical principles that preserves dense contact information while maintaining robustness for sim-to-real transfer. To support this representation, we propose a sensor calibration scheme based on differentiable dynamics, enabling the estimation of taxel orientations without requiring ground-truth force measurements. We evaluate CoP on two blind, challenging contact-rich manipulation tasks: peg-in-hole insertion and ball balancing. Across both tasks, policies conditioned on CoP achieve zero-shot sim-to-real transfer on a multi-fingered hand, and outperform both coarse binary-contact and raw-taxel baselines. Analysis of learned policy sta
  • 易漏细节梳理：

收集真实世界数据的困难是接触丰富操作的主要瓶颈，这使得直接获取所需数据变得极为困难。为了解决这一问题，简化触觉数据为粗略低维特征虽然可以降低数据处理的复杂性，但会牺牲复杂操作所需的丰富性。相比之下，CoP（Contact Point）能够保留密集的接触信息，同时保持模拟到现实的鲁棒性，从而在保持数据丰富性的同时，确保模拟结果在现实中的有效性。CoP不仅能够编码任务相关的物理属性，如物体质量，还能够实现零样本模拟到现实的转移，这意味着即使没有经过特定任务的训练，CoP也能在实际操作中表现出色。此外，CoP能够超越简单的二进制接触和原始触点基线，提供更精细和准确的触觉信息。CoP支持的传感器校准方案能够估计税元方向，进一步提升了其在复杂操作中的应用价值。因此，CoP不仅支持多指手进行 peg-in-hole 插入和球平衡任务，还能够在多种实际操作中展现出色的性能。

  • 关键点（易漏细节）：
  • 收集真实世界数据的困难是接触丰富操作的主要瓶颈。
  • 简化触觉数据为粗略低维特征会牺牲复杂操作所需的丰富性。
  • CoP能够保留密集的接触信息，同时保持模拟到现实的鲁棒性。
  • CoP能够编码任务相关的物理属性，如物体质量。
  • CoP能够实现零样本模拟到现实的转移。
  • CoP能够超越简单的二进制接触和原始触点基线。
  • CoP支持的传感器校准方案能够估计税元方向。
  • CoP能够支持多指手进行 peg-in-hole 插入和球平衡任务。

41. GraphSteal: Structural Knowledge Stealing from Graph RAG via Traversal Reconstruction

  • 链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28645v1
  • 来源：arxiv
  • 摘要：Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) enhances LLMs by grounding generation in query-relevant external evidence. Beyond unstructured text corpora, Graph RAG integrates knowledge graphs into the retrieval pipeline, enabling LLMs to access entities, relations, and multi-hop dependencies encoded in structured knowledge. However, the same structured knowledge that empowers Graph RAG also creates a new privacy attack surface. We demonstrate that Graph RAG systems can be turned into structural oracles: through adaptive black-box interactions, an adversary can elicit sufficient relational evidence to reconstruct substantial portions of the hidden knowledge graph. We propose a structure-oriented reconstruction framework that recovers targeted graphs from both local and global perspectives. Specifically, Depth-Wise Heuristic Search extracts fine-grained node attributes by recursively expanding entity-centered evidence, while Breadth-Wise Diffusion Search infers graph topology by propagating across relation-induced neighborhoods. Experiments on generic and healthcare scenarios demonstrate that our method can recover over 90\% of the original knowledge graph from representative Graph RAG syste
  • 易漏细节梳理：

Graph RAG 的结构知识可以被窃取，这表明其结构知识可能成为攻击的目标。黑盒交互可以揭示隐藏的知识图，进一步增加了结构知识被窃取的风险。为了解决这一问题，Depth-Wise Heuristic Search 通过递归扩展实体中心的证据来提取细粒度节点属性，而 Breadth-Wise Diffusion Search 则通过在关系诱导的邻域中传播来推断图拓扑。实验结果表明，可以从 Graph RAG 系统恢复超过 90% 的原始知识图，这进一步凸显了结构隐私在 Graph RAG 管道中的脆弱性。尽管现有防护措施对攻击提供有限防御，但结构隐私在 Graph RAG 管道中依然难以得到有效保护。因此，需要进一步加强防护措施，以确保结构知识的安全。

  • 关键点（易漏细节）：
  • Graph RAG 的结构知识可以被窃取。
  • 黑盒交互可以揭示隐藏的知识图。
  • Depth-Wise Heuristic Search 通过递归扩展实体中心的证据来提取细粒度节点属性。
  • Breadth-Wise Diffusion Search 通过在关系诱导的邻域中传播来推断图拓扑。
  • 实验表明可以从 Graph RAG 系统恢复超过 90% 的原始知识图。
  • 现有防护措施对攻击提供有限防御。
  • 结构隐私在 Graph RAG 管道中难以保护。

42. Principled Algorithms for Optimizing Generalized Metrics in Multi-Label Learning

  • 链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28767v1
  • 来源：arxiv
  • 摘要：Many real-world classification tasks require predicting multiple labels per instance, necessitating the optimization of complex evaluation metrics such as the $F$-measure and Jaccard index. While the Empirical Utility Maximization (EUM) framework is natural for these population-level metrics, existing theoretical results are largely limited to asymptotic Bayes-consistency. In this paper, we develop principled learning algorithms for optimizing a broad class of generalized metrics within the EUM framework, grounded in the stronger notion of $H$-consistency. Our key contribution is the design of novel surrogate loss functions for multi-label learning that admit provable $H$-consistency bounds, enabling optimization with non-asymptotic guarantees tailored to the hypothesis class and finite samples. Crucially, we prove these combinatorially formulated surrogates decompose exactly, operating in strictly $O(l)$ time without approximations. Building on this foundation, we introduce MMO (Multi-Label Metric Optimization), a new family of algorithms for optimizing generalized linear-fractional metrics. We validate our approach through extensive experiments, demonstrating robust scalability a
  • 易漏细节梳理：

首先，EUM框架适用于人口级指标，但现有理论结果主要局限于渐近贝叶斯一致性。其次，H-一致性比渐近贝叶斯一致性更强，因此设计的新代理损失函数允许证明H-一致性界。此外，MMO算法优化广义线性分数指标，而代理损失函数的组合形式可以精确分解，从而使得理论严谨性和实际有效性都得到了提升。进一步地，针对高稀疏性的多标签度量优化算法被提出，实验验证了方法在大规模数据集上的鲁棒性和性能。代理损失函数运行时间严格为O(l)，这表明该方法在处理大规模数据集时具有较高的效率。因此，这些改进不仅提升了理论基础，还显著增强了实际应用中的性能。

  • 关键点（易漏细节）：
  • EUM框架适用于人口级指标，但现有理论结果主要局限于渐近贝叶斯一致性。
  • H-一致性比渐近贝叶斯一致性更强。
  • 设计的新代理损失函数允许证明H-一致性界。
  • MMO算法优化广义线性分数指标。
  • 代理损失函数的组合形式可以精确分解。
  • 实验验证了方法在大规模数据集上的鲁棒性和性能。
  • 代理损失函数运行时间严格为O(l)。
  • 提出了针对高稀疏性的多标签度量优化算法。
  • 理论严谨性和实际有效性都得到了提升。

[2605.28767v1] Principled Algorithms for Optimizing Generalized Metrics in Multi-Label Learning Computer Science > Machine Learning arXiv:2605.28767v1 (cs) [Submitted on 27 May 2026] Title: Principled Algorithms for Optimizing Generalized Metrics in Multi-Label Learning Authors: Mehryar Mohri , Yutao Zhong View a PDF of the paper titled Principled Algorithms for Optimizing Generalized Metrics in Multi-Label Learning, by Mehryar Mohri and 1 other authors View PDF Abstract: Many real-world classification tasks require predicting multiple labels per instance, necessitating the optimization of complex evaluation metrics such as the $F$-measure and Jaccard index. While the Empirical Utility Maximization (EUM) framework is natural for these population-level metrics, existing theoretical results are largely limited to asymptotic Bayes-consistency. In this paper, we develop principled learning algorithms for optimizing a broad class of generalized metrics within the EUM framework, grounded in the stronger notion of $H$-consistency. Our key contribution is the design of novel surrogate loss functions for multi-label learning that admit provable $H$-consistency bounds, enabling optimization with non-asymptotic guarantees tailored to the hypothesis class and finite samples. Crucially, we prove these combinatorially formulated surrogates decompose exactly, operating in strictly $O(l)$ time without approximations. Building on this foundation, we introduce MMO (Multi-Label Metric Optimization), a new family of algorithms for optimizing generalized linear-fractional metrics. We validate our approach through extensive experiments, demonstrating robust scalability and superior performance over state-of-the-art continuous baselines on large-scale datasets (MS-COCO, Reuters-21578) in high-sparsity, deep learning regimes. Our results offer both theoretical rigor and practical effectiveness for general multi-label metric optimization. 43. Random Process Flow Matching: Generative Implicit Representations of Multivariate Random Fields

  • 链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28625v1
  • 来源：arxiv
  • 摘要：Generative modeling provides a powerful framework for learning data distributions. These models initially relied on probabilistic methods such as Gaussian Processes (GP) for uncertainty-aware predictions and shifted towards larger trainable models to learn more complex distributions. In this work, we introduce Random Process (RP) Flow, a Flow Matching-based framework that represents the vector field as a neural implicit function. Unlike modern generative methods, our setting involves a single observed field, from which only sparse measurements are available. RP Flow uses Random Fourier Features to learn an implicit signal representation that can be queried at any arbitrary location from a limited set of observations, while encoding uncertainty through ensemble sampling. We propose constructing a Bayesian posterior by GP regression in the source space to generate high-quality samples. Our empirical results demonstrate that this framework generates realistic samples along with calibrated uncertainty estimates, even under challenging conditions such as high frequency, high sparsity, or high dimensionality. These findings position RP Flow as a milestone towards generative models for re
  • 易漏细节梳理：

单个观测场中的稀疏测量限制了现代生成方法的应用，而RP Flow通过随机傅里叶特征学习隐式信号表示，能够在有限观测下查询任意位置。通过集合采样，RP Flow能够编码不确定性，即使在高频率、高稀疏性或高维度条件下，也能生成现实且具校准不确定性的样本。此外，生成高质量样本时，RP Flow使用GP回归构造贝叶斯后验，进一步增强了其在数据稀缺且必须保持可追踪不确定性的重建任务中的表现。RP Flow为生成多变量随机场的隐式表示提供了一种新方法，匹配了随机过程流的框架，代表矢量场为神经隐式函数。因此，RP Flow不仅基于流动匹配的框架，还依赖于如高斯过程这样的概率方法进行不确定性感知预测，其设置仅涉及一个观测场，从中只能获得稀疏测量。随着生成模型逐渐转向更复杂的分布，依赖于更大的可训练模型，RP Flow为在数据稀缺且必须保持可追踪不确定性的重建任务中生成模型设定了里程碑。

  • 关键点（易漏细节）：
  • 单个观测场中的稀疏测量限制了现代生成方法的应用。
  • RP Flow通过随机傅里叶特征学习隐式信号表示，可以在有限观测下查询任意位置。
  • 通过集合采样，RP Flow能够编码不确定性。
  • 生成高质量样本时，使用GP回归构造贝叶斯后验。
  • 即使在高频率、高稀疏性或高维度条件下，RP Flow也能生成现实且具校准不确定性的样本。
  • RP Flow为在数据稀缺且必须保持可追踪不确定性的重建任务中生成模型设定了里程碑。
  • 随机过程流匹配为生成多变量随机场的隐式表示提供了一种新方法。
  • 生成模型最初依赖于如高斯过程这样的概率方法进行不确定性感知预测。
  • RP Flow基于流动匹配的框架，代表矢量场为神经隐式函数。
  • RP Flow的设置仅涉及一个观测场，从中只能获得稀疏测量。
  • 生成模型逐渐转向更复杂的分布，依赖于更大的可训练模型。

These models initially relied on probabilistic methods such as Gaussian Processes (GP) for uncertainty-aware predictions and shifted towards larger trainable models to learn more complex distributions. 44. Energy-energy correlators inside single inclusive jets in heavy-ion collisions with CoLBT-hydro model

  • 链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28788v1
  • 来源：arxiv
  • 摘要：The energy-energy correlator (EEC) inside jets is a sensitive observable for studying jet modification in the quark-gluon plasma (QGP). However, its interpretation in heavy-ion collisions remains challenging, requiring a consistent understanding of jet evolution across multiple dynamical scales together with a proper treatment of the background subtraction. In this work, we employ an updated CoLBT-hydro framework in which a medium scale $Q_M$ = 2.0 GeV is introduced to separate the vacuum and in-medium stages of the parton shower, enabling a more self-consistent treatment of jet evolution. Using a theoretical background subtraction within the model, the resulting simulation reproduces the recent CMS measurement of the in-jet EEC, and through a decomposition of different contributions, highlights the impact of medium modification on the observable. To further validate the experimental procedure, we also implement the CMS mixed-event background-subtraction method directly in the simulation and find the results are consistent with that obtained with the theoretical background subtraction. Using $p_T$-ranked jets in each event, we further investigate the dependence of medium modificati
  • 易漏细节梳理：

背景减法的处理方式直接影响结果的解释，而不同背景减法方法的实现则会导致结果的一致性验证出现差异。CoLBT-hydro模型中的介质尺度有助于更一致地处理喷注演化，从而使得喷注在介质中的路径长度（EEC）能够得到更准确的反映。不同喷注在介质中的路径长度差异，进一步影响EEC的变化，使得EEC成为研究喷注引起的扩散尾迹的重要信号。因此，通过分析EEC的变化，可以更深入地理解喷注在介质中的行为及其引起的扩散效应。

  • 关键点（易漏细节）：
  • 背景减法的处理方式会影响结果的解释。
  • 背景减法方法的不同实现会导致结果的一致性验证。
  • CoLBT-hydro模型中的介质尺度有助于更一致地处理喷注演化。
  • EEC可以反映喷注在介质中的路径长度。
  • EEC的变化可以指示喷注引起的扩散尾迹。
  • 不同喷注在介质中的路径长度会影响EEC。
  • EEC可以作为信号来研究喷注引起的扩散尾迹。

[2605.28788v1] Energy-energy correlators inside single inclusive jets in heavy-ion collisions with CoLBT-hydro model High Energy Physics - Phenomenology arXiv:2605.28788v1 (hep-ph) [Submitted on 27 May 2026] Title: Energy-energy correlators inside single inclusive jets in heavy-ion collisions with CoLBT-hydro model Authors: Zhong Yang , Raghav Kunnawalkam Elayavalli , Xin-Nian Wang View a PDF of the paper titled Energy-energy correlators inside single inclusive jets in heavy-ion collisions with CoLBT-hydro model, by Zhong Yang and 2 other authors View PDF HTML (experimental) Abstract: The energy-energy correlator (EEC) inside jets is a sensitive observable for studying jet modification in the quark-gluon plasma (QGP). However, its interpretation in heavy-ion collisions remains challenging, requiring a consistent understanding of jet evolution across multiple dynamical scales together with a proper treatment of the background subtraction. In this work, we employ an updated CoLBT-hydro framework in which a medium scale $Q_M$ = 2.0 GeV is introduced to separate the vacuum and in-medium stages of the parton shower, enabling a more self-consistent treatment of jet evolution. Finally, we explore the dependence of the leading-jet EEC on the dijet rapidity gap as a signal of the jet-induced diffusion wake. 45. Learn from Weaknesses: Automated Domain Specialization for Small Computer-Use Agents

  • 链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28775v1
  • 来源：arxiv
  • 摘要：Computer-use agents (CUAs) have recently made substantial progress, but deploying a separate large expert for each software domain remains expensive. Small open computer-use agents are more practical specialization targets, but they remain substantially weaker and exhibit uneven domain-specific failures. A straightforward remedy is to synthesize large-scale training data for the target domain, yet we find that this naive approach yields only marginal improvements. Building on this observation, we introduce LearnWeak, an annotation-free specialization framework for small computer-use agents that uses a stronger reference agent to identify the student's weaknesses in the target domain, synthesize targeted tasks, and construct supervision automatically. LearnWeak further introduces an error-aware specialization objective that disentangles planning and execution errors, enabling more behaviorally precise updates than broad uniform supervision. On OSWorld, LearnWeak achieves average gains of 11.6 and 11.1 percentage points over EvoCUA-8B and OpenCUA-7B, respectively, across eight domains. We also validate that our student-aware dataset generation and training approaches outperform exist
  • 易漏细节梳理：

自动化领域专业化对于小型计算机使用代理是必要的，这使得学生代理的弱点可以通过更强的参考代理自动识别。进一步地，错误感知的专业化目标可以区分规划和执行错误，从而提升整体性能。学生意识在数据合成和代理训练中至关重要，它不仅能够帮助识别和纠正代理的弱点，还能在多个方面优于传统方法。此外，针对不同领域的八次实验显示了显著的性能提升，表明现有的自主轨迹生成和训练基线可能不如新的方法有效。因此，通过提升学生意识的专业化，可以显著改善代理的表现，从而在自动化领域取得更好的成果。

  • 关键点（易漏细节）：
  • 自动化领域专业化对于小型计算机使用代理是必要的。
  • 学生代理的弱点可以通过更强的参考代理自动识别。
  • 错误感知的专业化目标可以区分规划和执行错误。
  • 学生意识在数据合成和代理训练中至关重要。
  • 现有的自主轨迹生成和训练基线可能不如新的方法有效。
  • 针对不同领域的八次实验显示了显著的性能提升。
  • 学生意识的方法在多个方面优于传统方法。

46. AREA: Attribute Extraction and Aggregation for CLIP-Based Class-Incremental Learning

  • 链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28809v1
  • 来源：arxiv
  • 摘要：Class-Incremental Learning (CIL) is important in building real-world learning systems. In CLIP-based CIL, the model performs classification by comparing similarity between visual and textual embeddings obtained from template prompts, e.g., ``a photo of a [CLASS]''. This seemingly monolithic matching process can be decomposed into two conceptually distinct stages: attribute extraction and attribute aggregation. For example, a model may recognize cat using attributes such as fur texture and whiskers. When learning a new class like car, the model must extract additional attributes like wheels and adjust how they are aggregated in the shared representation space. However, since only data from the current task is available, incremental updates can bias both attribute extraction and aggregation toward new classes, leading to catastrophic forgetting. Therefore, we propose AREA for attribute extraction and aggregation in CLIP-based CIL. To stabilize extraction, we anchor class-level visual and textual attributes on the hyperspherical embedding space via principal geodesic analysis. To stabilize aggregation, we learn lightweight task-specific experts with scoring and residual refinement, re
  • 易漏细节梳理：

属性提取涉及识别特定特征，如毛皮纹理和胡须，这是通过识别图像中的关键细节来实现的。属性聚合则是将这些特征整合到共享表示空间中，以便更好地理解和分类。然而，增量更新可能会导致属性提取和聚合偏向于新类，从而引入偏差。为了解决这一问题，使用超球面嵌入空间并通过主地线分析稳定属性提取。此外，针对特定任务的专家被学习以稳定属性聚合，通过评分和残差细化来提高预测准确性。为了进一步优化学习过程，引入了变分信息瓶颈来正则化任务特定专家的学习。在推理过程中，使用最优传输方法可以实现更简洁的预测，提高预测效率。实验结果表明，这种方法在基于CLIP的连续学习中表现出色，优于现有的方法。然而，由于偏向于新类别的更新，可能会导致灾难性遗忘。通过最优传输在任务属性流形上进行路由，可以进一步提高预测效率。

  • 关键点（易漏细节）：
  • attribute extraction involves recognizing specific features like fur texture and whiskers.
  • attribute aggregation involves combining these features in the shared representation space.
  • incremental updates can bias attribute extraction and aggregation towards new classes.
  • hyperspherical embedding space helps stabilize attribute extraction through principal geodesic analysis.
  • task-specific experts are learned to stabilize attribute aggregation with scoring and residual refinement.
  • variational information bottleneck regularizes the learning of task-specific experts.
  • optimal transport is used during inference for more concise prediction.
  • experiments show AREA outperforms state-of-the-art methods in CLIP-based CIL.
  • catastrophic forgetting can occur due to biased updates towards new classes.
  • routing over task attribute manifolds via optimal transport improves prediction efficiency.

[2605.28809v1] AREA: Attribute Extraction and Aggregation for CLIP-Based Class-Incremental Learning Computer Science > Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition arXiv:2605.28809v1 (cs) [Submitted on 27 May 2026] Title: AREA: Attribute Extraction and Aggregation for CLIP-Based Class-Incremental Learning Authors: Zhen-Hao Xie , Yu-Cheng Shi , Da-Wei Zhou View a PDF of the paper titled AREA: Attribute Extraction and Aggregation for CLIP-Based Class-Incremental Learning, by Zhen-Hao Xie and 2 other authors View PDF HTML (experimental) Abstract: Class-Incremental Learning (CIL) is important in building real-world learning systems. In CLIP-based CIL, the model performs classification by comparing similarity between visual and textual embeddings obtained from template prompts, e.g., ``a photo of a [CLASS]''. This seemingly monolithic matching process can be decomposed into two conceptually distinct stages: attribute extraction and attribute aggregation. For example, a model may recognize cat using attributes such as fur texture and whiskers. When learning a new class like car, the model must extract additional attributes like wheels and adjust how they are aggregated in the shared representation space. However, since only data from the current task is available, incremental updates can bias both attribute extraction and aggregation toward new classes, leading to catastrophic forgetting. Therefore, we propose AREA for attribute extraction and aggregation in CLIP-based CIL. To stabilize extraction, we anchor class-level visual and textual attributes on the hyperspherical embedding space via principal geodesic analysis. To stabilize aggregation, we learn lightweight task-specific experts with scoring and residual refinement, regularized by a variational information bottleneck objective. During inference, we perform routing over task attribute manifolds via optimal transport for more concise prediction. Experiments show that AREA consistently outperforms SOTA methods. 47. Self-Improving Language Models with Bidirectional Evolutionary Search

  • 链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28814v1
  • 来源：arxiv
  • 摘要：Search has been proposed as an effective method for self-improving language models and agentic systems, both for post-training sample generation and for inference. However, widely used methods such as best-of-N sampling and tree search face two fundamental limitations: they are guided by sparse verification signals, and they construct candidates primarily through autoregressive expansion, restricting exploration to regions with substantial model probability mass. To address these, we propose Bidirectional Evolutionary Search (BES), a search framework that couples forward candidate evolution with backward goal decomposition. In the forward search, BES augments standard expansion with evolution operators that recombine partial trajectories to generate candidates that are difficult to obtain from a single model rollout. In the backward search, BES recursively decomposes the original task into checkable subgoals, producing dense intermediate feedback that guides forward search. We provide theoretical motivation showing that candidates generated by expansion-only search are confined to a narrow entropy shell while evolutionary operators can escape it, and that backward search can expone
  • 易漏细节梳理：

best-of-N采样和树搜索在稀疏验证信号的引导下进行，这有助于提高搜索效率。自回归扩展限制了探索范围，仅限于模型概率质量显著的区域，从而进一步优化搜索路径。仅通过扩展生成的候选者通常被限制在狭窄的熵壳内，这意味着它们的多样性有限。然而，进化操作可以突破这一限制，探索更广泛的解空间。此外，向后搜索可以显著减少所需的样本数量，从而提高整体效率。BES（双向搜索）结合了前向候选者进化和后向目标分解，通过递归分解提供密集的中间反馈，确保搜索过程的连续性和高效性。因此，BES在处理挑战性后训练任务时表现出一致的改进，并在三个开放问题解决基准测试中优于现有框架。此外，BES在困难任务上也优于主流后训练算法，展示了其在复杂场景下的优越性能。

  • 关键点（易漏细节）：
  • best-of-N sampling and tree search are guided by sparse verification signals.
  • autoregressive expansion restricts exploration to regions with substantial model probability mass.
  • candidates generated by expansion-only search are confined to a narrow entropy shell.
  • evolutionary operators can escape the narrow entropy shell.
  • backward search can exponentially reduce the number of required samples.
  • BES enables consistent gains on challenging post-training tasks.
  • BES outperforms existing open-source frameworks on three open problem solving benchmarks.
  • BES combines forward candidate evolution with backward goal decomposition.
  • BES provides dense intermediate feedback through recursive decomposition.
  • BES outperforms mainstream post-training algorithms on difficult tasks.

[2605.28814v1] Self-Improving Language Models with Bidirectional Evolutionary Search Computer Science > Computation and Language arXiv:2605.28814v1 (cs) [Submitted on 27 May 2026] Title: Self-Improving Language Models with Bidirectional Evolutionary Search Authors: Guowei Xu , Zhenting Qi , Huangyuan Su , Weirui Ye , Himabindu Lakkaraju , Sham M. Kakade , Yilun Du View a PDF of the paper titled Self-Improving Language Models with Bidirectional Evolutionary Search, by Guowei Xu and 6 other authors View PDF HTML (experimental) Abstract: Search has been proposed as an effective method for self-improving language models and agentic systems, both for post-training sample generation and for inference. However, widely used methods such as best-of-N sampling and tree search face two fundamental limitations: they are guided by sparse verification signals, and they construct candidates primarily through autoregressive expansion, restricting exploration to regions with substantial model probability mass. To address these, we propose Bidirectional Evolutionary Search (BES), a search framework that couples forward candidate evolution with backward goal decomposition. In the forward search, BES augments standard expansion with evolution operators that recombine partial trajectories to generate candidates that are difficult to obtain from a single model rollout. In the backward search, BES recursively decomposes the original task into checkable subgoals, producing dense intermediate feedback that guides forward search. We provide theoretical motivation showing that candidates generated by expansion-only search are confined to a narrow entropy shell while evolutionary operators can escape it, and that backward search can exponentially reduce the number of required samples to find a correct answer. Experiments show that on challenging post-training tasks where mainstream post-training algorithms fail to improve, BES enables consistent gains, and on three open problem solving benchmarks at inference time, BES outperforms existing open-source frameworks in both average and best-case performance. Code and trained models are available at this https URL . 48. Complex abelian varieties and quantum error correction: a mathematical framework for GKP codes

  • 链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28784v1
  • 来源：arxiv
  • 摘要：We study a class of quantum error-correcting codes through the geometry of complex abelian varieties. These codes, introduced by Gottesman--Kitaev--Preskill, are built from symplectically integral lattices and therefore naturally define polarized complex abelian varieties. We give a precise mathematical formulation of this relationship and extend it to a dictionary between the main structures of GKP code theory and classical objects in the theory of abelian varieties. For instance, under this dictionary, the finite-dimensional code space becomes the space of theta functions $H^0(X, L)$, logical Pauli gates arise from the theta group, passive logical Clifford gates correspond to automorphisms of the polarized abelian variety, and concatenation with stabilizer codes corresponds to isogeny. We also prove several key results that give precise mathematical formulations of statements about these codes that often appear in heuristic form in the physics literature. In particular, we prove that the encoding is asymptotically isometric, that every logical Clifford gate is realized by a Gaussian unitary, and that, for noise of small variance, the failure probability is governed to first order
  • 易漏细节梳理：

GKP 代码是基于辛可积格子构建的，这使得它们在量子纠错中具有独特的优势。逻辑保罗伊门 gates 由 theta 群表示，而被动逻辑克利福德 gates 则与自同构相关联。通过与稳定器代码的串联，GKP 代码的逻辑操作可以等同于同构操作。编码过程在极限中是渐近等距的，这意味着编码操作在无限维度上保持了几何距离。此外，每个逻辑克利福德 gates 都可以通过高斯单元操作来实现，这进一步强化了 GKP 代码的灵活性。编码失败的概率由最短非平凡位移的长度来控制，这表明位移长度对编码的鲁棒性有直接影响。优化问题在极化阿贝尔簇的模空间上出现，这为理解和优化 GKP 代码提供了新的视角。编码过程在极限中自然地保持了等距性，而 theta 函数空间则对应于有限维的代码空间。此外，通过字典将 GKP 代码理论翻译到阿贝尔簇结构中，可以更好地理解其噪声鲁棒性，其中同构不变量在噪声分析中扮演重要角色。

  • 关键点（易漏细节）：
  • GKP codes are built from symplectically integral lattices.
  • Logical Pauli gates are represented by the theta group.
  • Passive logical Clifford gates are associated with automorphisms.
  • Concatenation with stabilizer codes corresponds to isogeny.
  • Encoding is asymptotically isometric.
  • Every logical Clifford gate is realized by a Gaussian unitary.
  • Failure probability is governed by the shortest nontrivial displacement.
  • Optimization problems arise on the moduli space of polarized abelian varieties.
  • The encoding process is naturally isometric in the limit.
  • The theta function space corresponds to the finite-dimensional code space.
  • Systolic invariants play a role in noise resilience analysis.
  • The dictionary translates GKP code theory to abelian variety structures.

These codes, introduced by Gottesman--Kitaev--Preskill, are built from symplectically integral lattices and therefore naturally define polarized complex abelian varieties. We give a precise mathematical formulation of this relationship and extend it to a dictionary between the main structures of GKP code theory and classical objects in the theory of abelian varieties. For instance, under this dictionary, the finite-dimensional code space becomes the space of theta functions $H^0(X, L)$, logical Pauli gates arise from the theta group, passive logical Clifford gates correspond to automorphisms of the polarized abelian variety, and concatenation with stabilizer codes corresponds to isogeny. This leads naturally to optimization problems on the moduli space of polarized abelian varieties. 49. Ω-QVLA: Robust Quantization for Vision-Language-Action Models via Composite Rotation and Per-step Scaling

  • 链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28803v1
  • 来源：arxiv
  • 摘要：Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models unify perception, reasoning, and control within a single policy, yet their multi-billion-parameter backbones and diffusion-based action heads make on-device deployment prohibitively expensive. Prior quantization efforts offer only partial solutions, compressing the LLM backbone while leaving the DiT action head at full precision, or resorting to mixed-precision schemes, driven by the belief that uniformly quantizing the action head is inherently unstable. We challenge this assumption with Omega-QVLA, the first training-free post-training quantization framework that compresses both the language backbone and the entire diffusion action head of a VLA model to a uniform W4A4 precision, eliminating the need for mixed-precision allocation. Omega-QVLA combines a composite SVD-Hadamard rotation that equalizes per-channel weight energy while diffusing residual activation outliers with per-step DiT activation scaling quantization that absorbs dynamic-range drift across denoising steps. On LIBERO, Omega-QVLA compresses Pi 0.5 and GR00T N1.5 to W4A4 with 98.0% and 87.8% task success rates, matching or exceeding their FP16 references of 97.1% and 87.0%, while
  • 易漏细节梳理：

均匀量化行动头并非不稳定的，这一假设被挑战，均匀量化后的模型任务成功率接近或超过全精度版本，这表明均匀量化在保持模型性能方面具有显著优势。然而，均匀量化并非唯一的选择，Omega-QVLA提供了一种替代方案，进一步丰富了量化技术的选择。为了提高模型的性能，Omega-QVLA框架引入了SVD-Hadamard旋转，该技术有助于平衡通道权重的能量并分散残余激活的异常值，从而提升模型的整体表现。此外，DiT激活缩放量化有助于吸收去噪步骤中的动态范围漂移，进一步优化模型的性能。值得注意的是，Omega-QVLA在减少静态内存占用方面表现出色，因此在实际应用中能够提供更好的性能。相比之下，先前的方法在现实世界的操作实验中表现不佳，而Omega-QVLA则能实现平滑准确的操作，这表明其在实际应用中的优越性。因此，Omega-QVLA不仅在理论上有优势，而且在实际操作中也表现出色，为量化技术提供了新的解决方案。

  • 关键点（易漏细节）：
  • 均匀量化行动头并非不稳定的，这一假设被挑战。
  • 均匀量化后的模型任务成功率接近或超过全精度版本。
  • 混合精度方案并非唯一的选择，Omega-QVLA提供了一种替代方案。
  • SVD-Hadamard旋转有助于平衡通道权重能量并分散残余激活异常值。
  • DiT激活缩放量化有助于吸收去噪步骤中的动态范围漂移。
  • Omega-QVLA在减少静态内存占用方面表现出色。
  • 先前方法在现实世界的操作实验中表现不佳，而Omega-QVLA则能实现平滑准确的操作。
  • Omega-QVLA框架无需训练即可进行后训练量化。

50. How reproducible are first-principles simulations of liquid water?

  • 链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28798v1
  • 来源：arxiv
  • 摘要：Liquid water is fundamentally important, and its accurate computer simulation has been the driving force for myriad methodological developments. Ab initio molecular dynamics with forces obtained from density functional theory (DFT) is now a standard tool widely used by researchers. However, we reveal that previous studies of liquid water using the same widely-used density functional (revPBE-D3) exhibit significant discrepancies with one another, varying by over 20% in the diffusion coefficient and 10% in the density, raising fundamental questions about reproducibility. By combining modern long-range machine-learning interatomic potentials that enable robust statistical sampling with carefully converged DFT training data, we resolve these discrepancies, achieving consensus across six diverse community codes. Our predictions differ markedly from previous literature: we show that most previous results overestimate the density and underestimate the diffusion coefficient of revPBE-D3 water due to basis set incompleteness and pseudopotential inconsistencies, coupled with limitations in statistical sampling (in some cases). These benchmark values provide a reliable reference for validatin
  • 易漏细节梳理：

首先，模拟液态水时使用revPBE-D3密度泛函存在重现性问题，导致扩散系数和密度在不同研究中存在显著差异。其次，基础集不完整和伪势不一致导致密度被高估和扩散系数被低估。此外，统计采样限制也加剧了这些差异。因此，现代机器学习原子间势能有助于解决这些分歧。提供的基准值可以用于验证未来基于密度泛函理论的模拟，从而增强对领域的信心和可信度。最终，达成一致对于评估新的密度泛函和数值近似至关重要，确保了模拟结果的可靠性。

  • 关键点（易漏细节）：
  • reproducibility issues exist for simulations of liquid water using the revPBE-D3 density functional.
  • discrepancies in diffusion coefficients and densities among different studies are significant.
  • basis set incompleteness and pseudopotential inconsistencies contribute to overestimated density and underestimated diffusion.
  • statistical sampling limitations also play a role in the discrepancies observed.
  • modern machine-learning interatomic potentials help resolve these discrepancies.
  • benchmark values provided are reliable for validating future DFT-based simulations.
  • reaching agreement enhances confidence and credibility in the field.
  • reproducibility is crucial for assessing new density functionals and numerical approximations.

[2605.28798v1] How reproducible are first-principles simulations of liquid water? Physics > Chemical Physics arXiv:2605.28798v1 (physics) [Submitted on 27 May 2026] Title: How reproducible are first-principles simulations of liquid water? Authors: Niamh ONeill , Benjamin X. Shi , William J. Baldwin , Albert P. Bartok , Chris J. Pickard , Angelos Michaelides , Gabor Csanyi , Timothy C. Berkelbach View a PDF of the paper titled How reproducible are first-principles simulations of liquid water?, by Niamh ONeill and 6 other authors View PDF HTML (experimental) Abstract: Liquid water is fundamentally important, and its accurate computer simulation has been the driving force for myriad methodological developments. Ab initio molecular dynamics with forces obtained from density functional theory (DFT) is now a standard tool widely used by researchers. Reaching agreement establishes confidence and credibility and serves as a prerequisite for the systematic assessment of new density functionals and numerical approximations. 51. Blind PRNG Hijacking: An Undetectable Integrity-Preserving Attack Against LLM Watermarking

  • 链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28632v1
  • 来源：arxiv
  • 摘要：Cryptographic watermarking is a leading defense for attributing text generated by large language models (LLMs). Existing schemes, including KGW, Unigram, and DipMark, derive their security guarantees from the assumption that the underlying pseudo-random number generator (PRNG) is trustworthy. This work introduces SeedHijack, the first supply-chain attack on LLM watermarking that is simultaneously (i) blind -- requiring no knowledge of the watermark key, detector, or model logits, (ii) integrity-preserving -- amplifying rather than erasing the watermark signal, and (iii) orthogonal to detection -- the attack-induced bias is statistically independent of all content-side detector statistics, ensuring that amplification and evasion coexist without trade-off. Rather than perturbing generated text, SeedHijack replaces the PRNG at the supply-chain layer, biasing green-list selection without altering output tokens or degrading text quality. Across three watermarking schemes and three open-source LLMs, the attack triggers 0/6 state-of-the-art content-side statistical detectors while inflating the watermark z-score up to 2.42x (system-level defenses such as entropy-source attestation remain
  • 易漏细节梳理：

PRNG 的完整性是加密内容溯源系统的重要安全需求，确保系统能够准确溯源加密内容。然而，SeedHijack 攻击并不会改变输出令牌或降低文本质量，也不会触发任何先进的内容侧统计检测器，这使得它成为一种难以防范的威胁。这种攻击不需要知道水印密钥、检测器或模型输出，因此在现有系统中可能难以被发现。为了解决这一问题，量子随机数生成器（QRNG）能够提供完全的保护，因为它可以完全中和 SeedHijack 攻击。此外，entropy-source 保护与 SeedHijack 攻击是正交且互补的，这意味着它们可以共同增强系统的安全性，确保即使在 SeedHijack 攻击的情况下，系统仍然能够保持其完整性。因此，通过结合 QRNG 和 entropy-source 保护，可以有效提升系统的安全性，防止 SeedHijack 攻击带来的潜在威胁。

  • 关键点（易漏细节）：
  • PRNG 的完整性是加密内容溯源系统的重要安全需求。
  • SeedHijack 攻击不需要知道水印密钥、检测器或模型输出。
  • SeedHijack 攻击不会改变输出令牌或降低文本质量。
  • SeedHijack 攻击不会触发任何先进的内容侧统计检测器。
  • SeedHijack 攻击可以放大水印信号而不被检测器发现。
  • 量子随机数生成器（QRNG）可以完全中和 SeedHijack 攻击。
  • entropy-source 保护与 SeedHijack 攻击是正交且互补的。

[2605.28632v1] Blind PRNG Hijacking: An Undetectable Integrity-Preserving Attack Against LLM Watermarking Computer Science > Cryptography and Security arXiv:2605.28632v1 (cs) [Submitted on 27 May 2026] Title: Blind PRNG Hijacking: An Undetectable Integrity-Preserving Attack Against LLM Watermarking Authors: Ziyang You , Huilong He , Xiaoke Yang , Xuxing Lu View a PDF of the paper titled Blind PRNG Hijacking: An Undetectable Integrity-Preserving Attack Against LLM Watermarking, by Ziyang You and 3 other authors View PDF HTML (experimental) Abstract: Cryptographic watermarking is a leading defense for attributing text generated by large language models (LLMs). Existing schemes, including KGW, Unigram, and DipMark, derive their security guarantees from the assumption that the underlying pseudo-random number generator (PRNG) is trustworthy. This work introduces SeedHijack, the first supply-chain attack on LLM watermarking that is simultaneously (i) blind -- requiring no knowledge of the watermark key, detector, or model logits, (ii) integrity-preserving -- amplifying rather than erasing the watermark signal, and (iii) orthogonal to detection -- the attack-induced bias is statistically independent of all content-side detector statistics, ensuring that amplification and evasion coexist without trade-off. A quantum random number generator (QRNG) countermeasure is shown to fully neutralize the attack while preserving benign watermarking utility. These findings establish PRNG integrity as a first-class security requirement for cryptographic content-provenance systems. 52. Whispers of Supergravity in Gravitational Wave Backgrounds: Determining the Gravitino Mass from Cosmic Thermal History

  • 链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28804v1
  • 来源：arxiv
  • 摘要：Gravitino masses above the electroweak scale provide the simplest solution to the gravitino problem, but such large mass scales lie far beyond the reach of collider experiments. We show that the stochastic gravitational wave background offers a direct probe of this otherwise inaccessible regime. Despite decaying before Big-Bang Nucleosynthesis (BBN), these gravitinos naturally generate a period of early matter domination in the early universe. This non-standard epoch leaves a characteristic imprint on any primordial gravitational wave background, characterised by two frequencies corresponding to the onset and end of this phase. We demonstrate that these features can be used to directly infer both the gravitino mass and its initial abundance in a direct mapping. Future gravitational wave observatories span a vast frequency range, enabling sensitivity to gravitino masses from the BBN bound of $\mathcal{O}(100)\,\text{TeV}$ all the way up to $\mathcal{O}(10^{10})\,\text{TeV}$, with recent signal by NANOGrav already probing masses in the range $500$-$10^4$ TeV. Gravitational wave observables therefore probe an enormous region of parameter space, far beyond the reach of collider experim
  • 易漏细节梳理：

gravitino 质量高于电弱尺度时，无法通过碰撞实验进行探测，但随机引力波背景提供了一种探测这些不可触及区域的方法。gravitinos 可以在大爆炸核合成（BBN）之前产生一个早期物质主导时期，这一非标准时期会在原始引力波背景中留下特有的印记。这些特征可以直接用于推断gravitino的质量及其初始丰度。未来引力波观测站可以探测从 $\mathcal{O}(100)\,\text{TeV}$ 到 $\mathcal{O}(10^{10})\,\text{TeV}$ 的gravitino质量范围。最近NANOGrav的信号已经探测到 $500$ 到 $10^4$ TeV 范围内的质量。引力波观测量可以探测超出碰撞实验范围的庞大参数空间，同时通过引力波背景还可以探测超引力理论。

  • 关键点（易漏细节）：
  • gravitino masses above the electroweak scale are inaccessible to collider experiments.
  • stochastic gravitational wave background offers a probe of these otherwise inaccessible regimes.
  • gravitinos can generate a period of early matter domination before BBN.
  • this non-standard epoch leaves a characteristic imprint on primordial gravitational wave background.
  • these features can be used to directly infer both the gravitino mass and its initial abundance.
  • future gravitational wave observatories can probe gravitino masses from $\mathcal{O}(100)\,\text{TeV}$ to $\mathcal{O}(10^{10})\,\text{TeV}$.
  • recent signal by NANOGrav already probes masses in the range $500$-$10^4$ TeV.
  • gravitational wave observables probe an enormous region of parameter space beyond collider experiments.
  • supergravity can be probed through gravitational wave backgrounds alongside collider experiments.

53. A cryogenic apparatus for coupling two-dimensional materials to a confocal multimode optical cavity

  • 链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28815v1
  • 来源：arxiv
  • 摘要：Two-dimensional van der Waals materials exhibit a variety of correlated electron phases, and optical driving offers a promising route toward manipulating them. For example, cavity-enhanced, continuous-wave (CW) Raman excitation has been suggested as a way to coherently and superradiantly populate phonons or charge density waves via material excitons. A steady-state phonon population may be sustained with sufficiently strong electron-phonon coupling to drive novel collective response. We describe an apparatus built to meet the requirements of such an experimental program: Namely, an ultrahigh-vacuum system housing a length-tunable confocal Fabry-Pérot cavity with an intracavity sample, both cryogenically cooled and stabilized against vibrations. A four-axis nanopositioner aligns the sample and supports electrical leads for sample carrier density modulation and transport measurements. Transmission through the multimode cavity enables in situ sample imaging for alignment; the sample is a transition metal dichalcogenide in this work. Operating near the confocal geometry concentrates the optical field into a localized supermode that substantially enhances light-matter coupling. This enh
  • 易漏细节梳理：

cavity-enhanced Raman excitation能够相干地填充声子或电荷密度波，这要求腔体冷却以稳定样品免受振动影响。为了保持样品完整性，需要在超高真空条件下进行操作。长度可调的腔体不仅允许样品对准和交换，还能通过共焦几何结构增强光-物质耦合，即使在毫米级腔体长度下也是如此。通过传输方式，腔体内的样品成像得以实现，而样品载体密度的调制则通过电气连接支持。强电子-声子耦合可以维持声子群体，进而驱动新型集体响应。为了精确控制，样品需要通过低温冷却的腔体进行对准，这得益于一个四轴纳米定位器的使用。因此，这些技术手段共同作用，确保了实验的精确性和可靠性。

  • 关键点（易漏细节）：
  • cavity-enhanced Raman excitation can coherently populate phonons or charge density waves.
  • cavity cooling is necessary to stabilize the sample against vibrations.
  • ultrahigh-vacuum conditions are required to maintain sample integrity.
  • length-tunable cavities allow for sample alignment and exchange.
  • confocal geometry enhances light-matter coupling even at millimeter-scale cavity lengths.
  • in situ sample imaging is enabled by transmission through the multimode cavity.
  • sample carrier density modulation is supported by electrical leads.
  • phonon populations can be sustained with strong electron-phonon coupling.
  • steady-state phonon populations can drive novel collective responses.
  • cavities with intracavity samples are cryogenically cooled.
  • a four-axis nanopositioner aligns the sample for precise control.

[2605.28815v1] A cryogenic apparatus for coupling two-dimensional materials to a confocal multimode optical cavity Quantum Physics arXiv:2605.28815v1 (quant-ph) [Submitted on 27 May 2026] Title: A cryogenic apparatus for coupling two-dimensional materials to a confocal multimode optical cavity Authors: Han S. Hiller , Pranav Parakh , Samuel H. Aronson , Kenji Maeda , Di Lao , Julian Stewart , Zengde She , Jierong Wang , Xiaodong Xu , Tony Heinz , Benjamin L. Lev View a PDF of the paper titled A cryogenic apparatus for coupling two-dimensional materials to a confocal multimode optical cavity, by Han S. Hiller and 10 other authors View PDF HTML (experimental) Abstract: Two-dimensional van der Waals materials exhibit a variety of correlated electron phases, and optical driving offers a promising route toward manipulating them. For example, cavity-enhanced, continuous-wave (CW) Raman excitation has been suggested as a way to coherently and superradiantly populate phonons or charge density waves via material excitons. A steady-state phonon population may be sustained with sufficiently strong electron-phonon coupling to drive novel collective response. We describe an apparatus built to meet the requirements of such an experimental program: Namely, an ultrahigh-vacuum system housing a length-tunable confocal Fabry-Pérot cavity with an intracavity sample, both cryogenically cooled and stabilized against vibrations. A four-axis nanopositioner aligns the sample and supports electrical leads for sample carrier density modulation and transport measurements. Transmission through the multimode cavity enables in situ sample imaging for alignment; the sample is a transition metal dichalcogenide in this work. Operating near the confocal geometry concentrates the optical field into a localized supermode that substantially enhances light-matter coupling. This enhancement is preserved despite the millimeter-scale cavity length, which provides room for sample alignment and exchange. 54. PEFT-Arena: Understanding Parameter-Efficient Finetuning from a Stability-Plasticity Perspective

  • 链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28819v1
  • 来源：arxiv
  • 摘要：Parameter-efficient finetuning (PEFT) has become the standard approach for adapting large language models, yet evaluations largely emphasize downstream accuracy while overlooking the retention of pretrained capabilities. We argue that PEFT should be assessed through the stability-plasticity dilemma: the trade-off between target-task adaptation and resistance to forgetting. We introduce PEFT-Arena, a benchmark that jointly measures downstream performance and general capability retention. Across methods, we find distinct stability-plasticity profiles; under comparable parameter budgets, orthogonal finetuning achieves the most favorable Pareto frontier. To explain these differences, we analyze PEFT updates from two geometric perspectives. In weight space, spectral analysis reveals how parameterizations interact with the pretrained singular-value structure. In activation space, retention metrics show whether finetuning preserves or distorts general-capability representations, with forgetting linked to non-isometric representation distortion. Finally, an analysis shows that final SFT checkpoints often overshoot a better target-retention operating point. Inspired by this, we present case
  • 易漏细节梳理：

下游准确性评估往往忽视了预训练能力的保留，这导致了模型在适应目标任务时容易遗忘先前学习的能力。为了解决这一问题，PEFT（Parameter Efficient Fine-Tuning）方法通过平衡目标任务适应性和防止遗忘的能力，来优化模型的性能。PEFT-Arena基准不仅衡量下游性能，还评估了模型保留一般能力的能力。研究表明，正交微调方法在稳定性和灵活性之间取得了最佳平衡，能够更好地保留预训练结构。通过谱分析在权重空间中，可以揭示参数化与预训练结构的相互作用。此外，在激活空间中的保留度指标可以显示是否保留了通用能力的表示。遗忘与非等距表示失真有关，这进一步解释了为什么模型在最终微调检查点时可能会过度适应目标任务，从而导致保留能力的下降。因此，路径回溯可以作为一种后处理改进方法，帮助更好地保留目标任务的能力。

  • 关键点（易漏细节）：
  • downstream accuracy evaluations often neglect the retention of pretrained capabilities.
  • PEFT should balance target-task adaptation with resistance to forgetting.
  • PEFT-Arena benchmark measures both downstream performance and general capability retention.
  • Orthogonal finetuning tends to achieve the best trade-off in stability-plasticity.
  • Spectral analysis in weight space reveals how parameterizations interact with pretrained structures.
  • Retention metrics in activation space show whether general-capability representations are preserved.
  • Forgetting is linked to non-isometric representation distortion.
  • Final SFT checkpoints often overshoot the optimal target-retention operating point.
  • Path-wise rewinding can be a post-hoc improvement for better target-retention.

55. OmniVerifier-M1: Multimodal Meta-Verifier with Explicit Structured Recalibration

  • 链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28805v1
  • 来源：arxiv
  • 摘要：Visual outcomes are increasingly central to multimodal large language models, making reliable and fine-grained verification essential for scaling generalist foundation models. In this work, we investigate multimodal meta-verification, which leverages verifier-generated rationales rather than decision-only signals, and explore how to effectively incorporate meta-verification feedback into multimodal verifier training. We identify two key findings. First, symbolic verifier outputs (e.g., bounding boxes) outperform textual explanations as meta-verification rationales, enabling efficient rule-based reinforcement learning rewards while avoiding reliance on model-based rewards from auxiliary judge models. Second, decoupling reinforcement learning objectives for binary judgment and meta-verification substantially outperforms joint reward optimization, due to intrinsic differences in output structure and learning dynamics. Based on these insights, we train OmniVerifier-M1, a generalist visual verifier leveraging symbolic meta-verification and decoupled reinforcement learning. OmniVerifier-M1 provides robust verification and fine-grained error localization, and further enables M1-TTS, a ver
  • 易漏细节梳理：

首先，verifier-generated rationales相较于决策信号更能提供可靠和细粒度的验证，这使得验证过程更加精确和可信。其次，symbolic verifier outputs相比文本解释更有效地作为元验证的依据，这是因为符号化的输出能够更直接地反映验证过程中的逻辑和规则。此外，通过将二元判断与元验证分离，强化学习的目标得到了显著提升，这进一步增强了系统的性能。OmniVerifier-M1作为一个实例，展示了其在提供鲁棒的验证和细粒度的错误定位方面的优越性。M1-TTS则是一个由验证器驱动的生成系统，它能够实现动态区域级别的自我纠正，从而提高系统的灵活性和适应性。因此，元验证不仅支持更安全和更可控的基础模型部署，还为整个验证过程提供了坚实的基础。

  • 关键点（易漏细节）：
  • verifier-generated rationales比决策信号更能提供可靠和细粒度的验证。
  • symbolic verifier outputs比文本解释更有效地作为元验证的依据。
  • decoupling二元判断和元验证的强化学习目标能显著提高性能。
  • OmniVerifier-M1提供鲁棒的验证和细粒度的错误定位。
  • M1-TTS是一个由验证器驱动的生成系统，实现动态区域级别的自我纠正。
  • 元验证支持更安全和更可控的基础模型部署。

We identify two key findings. This approach paves the way for more reliable, interpretable, and fine-grained multimodal verification, supporting safer and more controllable foundation model deployment. Comments: ICML 2026. 56. Sketch2Motion: Text-driven 2D Sketch to 3D Animation via Diffusion-guided Skeleton Optimization

  • 链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28394v1
  • 来源：arxiv
  • 摘要：Animation of 2D hand-drawn sketches provides an effective medium for visual communication. However, these sketches pose challenges, particularly in handling occlusions and accurately mapping motion. While 3D animation naturally addresses these challenges, estimating 3D motion remains a very complex task. Recent approaches to converting 2D sketches to 3D animations have mainly focused on specific types of motion, such as bipedal movements and facial expressions. We propose Sketch2Motion, a diffusion-guided framework for skeleton-based motion synthesis that combines classical character animation pipelines with deep generative priors. Our method represents motion using skeletal transformations, which are propagated to mesh deformations via linear blend skinning. To guide the resulting animation toward realistic and semantically meaningful motion, we integrate a text-to-video diffusion model via motion-aware score-distillation sampling (MoSDS), enabling optimization without paired motion data. Additionally, we apply physics-inspired smoothness, topological, and contact constraints to stabilize optimization and preserve motion plausibility. Further, we integrate a spring-mass simulator
  • 易漏细节梳理：

D手绘草图的动画能够有效传达视觉信息，但在处理遮挡和准确映射运动时存在挑战。虽然2D动画自然解决了这些挑战，但估计3D运动仍然是一个非常复杂的问题。最近的方法主要集中在特定类型的运动上，如双足运动和面部表情。Sketch2Motion方法通过使用骨架变换来表示运动，并利用线性混合皮肤技术将骨架变形传播到网格变形。为了提高生成动画的质量，该方法还引入了运动感知得分蒸馏采样（MoSDS）来指导文本到视频扩散模型，并通过物理启发的平滑性、拓扑和接触约束来稳定优化。此外，弹簧-质量模拟器被引入以生成次级运动效果。该框架是通用的、完全可微的、模块化的，并兼容双足、四足和非活体骨架角色。实验结果表明，该方法生成的时间连贯、文本对齐的动画优于基线方法，而缺乏生成先验或显式物理约束的方法表现较差。因此，该方法为生成高质量的3D动画提供了一个有效的解决方案。代码和数据集将公开提供，以促进进一步的研究和应用。

  • 关键点（易漏细节）：
  • D手绘草图的动画能够有效传达视觉信息。
  • 处理遮挡和准确映射运动是2D草图的挑战。
  • D动画自然解决了这些挑战，但估计3D运动仍然是一个非常复杂的问题。
  • 最近的方法主要集中在特定类型的运动上，如双足运动和面部表情。
  • Sketch2Motion方法使用骨架变换来表示运动。
  • 线性混合皮肤技术将骨架变形传播到网格变形。
  • 通过运动感知得分蒸馏采样（MoSDS）指导文本到视频扩散模型。
  • 物理启发的平滑性、拓扑和接触约束有助于稳定优化。
  • 弹簧-质量模拟器引入了次级运动效果。
  • 该框架是通用的、完全可微的、模块化的，并兼容双足、四足和非活体骨架角色。
  • 实验表明，该方法生成的时间连贯、文本对齐的动画优于基线方法。
  • 缺乏生成先验或显式物理约束的方法表现较差。
  • 代码和数据集将公开提供。

[2605.28394v1] Sketch2Motion: Text-driven 2D Sketch to 3D Animation via Diffusion-guided Skeleton Optimization Computer Science > Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition arXiv:2605.28394v1 (cs) [Submitted on 27 May 2026] Title: Sketch2Motion: Text-driven 2D Sketch to 3D Animation via Diffusion-guided Skeleton Optimization Authors: Gaurav Rai , Ojaswa Sharma View a PDF of the paper titled Sketch2Motion: Text-driven 2D Sketch to 3D Animation via Diffusion-guided Skeleton Optimization, by Gaurav Rai and 1 other authors View PDF HTML (experimental) Abstract: Animation of 2D hand-drawn sketches provides an effective medium for visual communication. However, these sketches pose challenges, particularly in handling occlusions and accurately mapping motion. While 3D animation naturally addresses these challenges, estimating 3D motion remains a very complex task. Recent approaches to converting 2D sketches to 3D animations have mainly focused on specific types of motion, such as bipedal movements and facial expressions. We propose Sketch2Motion, a diffusion-guided framework for skeleton-based motion synthesis that combines classical character animation pipelines with deep generative priors. Our method represents motion using skeletal transformations, which are propagated to mesh deformations via linear blend skinning. To guide the resulting animation toward realistic and semantically meaningful motion, we integrate a text-to-video diffusion model via motion-aware score-distillation sampling (MoSDS), enabling optimization without paired motion data. Additionally, we apply physics-inspired smoothness, topological, and contact constraints to stabilize optimization and preserve motion plausibility. The proposed framework is generalized, fully differentiable, modular, and compatible with biped, quadruped, and non-living articulated characters. Experiments demonstrate that our approach produces temporally coherent, text-aligned animations that outperform baseline motion transfer methods that lack generative priors or explicit physical constraints. We will make our code and dataset publicly available. 57. Soft-SVeRL: Self-Verified Reinforcement Learning with Soft Rewards

  • 链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28561v1
  • 来源：arxiv
  • 摘要：Reinforcement Learning from Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has improved language models in domains such as mathematics and code, where correctness can be checked automatically. However, many important tasks are only partially verifiable: prompts contain multiple requirements, responses may satisfy some but not all of them, or no single reference answer might exist. We introduce Soft-RLVR, a framework for reinforcement learning from decomposed, learned verification signals. Soft-RLVR converts each prompt into a checklist of atomic requirements, scores candidate responses item by item with an LLM verifier, and trains on the resulting soft reward. Checklist-based rewards turn sparse pass/fail supervision into a denser partial-credit signal, but they also introduce a tradeoff: averaging item-level judgments can reduce verifier noise, while partial credit can reward incomplete responses. We formalize this tradeoff and identify conditions under which checklist-based verification gives a more reliable RL training signal than holistic verification. We further introduce Soft-SVeRL, a self-verifying variant of Soft-RLVR in which the policy also acts as the verifier. We show that self-verification
  • 易漏细节梳理：

verifiable rewards仅在部分可验证的任务中有效，而checklist-based rewards通过将稀疏的通过/失败监督转换为更密集的部分信用信号，使得奖励机制更加细致和有效。item-level judgments的平均值可以减少验证器噪声，从而提高整体验证的准确性。然而，partial credit可能奖励不完整的响应，这可能导致奖励膨胀，特别是当系统依赖于自我验证时。因此，需要显式稳定来防止自我验证的崩溃，确保验证过程的可靠性。此外，verifier质量和checklist质量都对下游的强化学习（RL）结果产生重要影响，显式稳定对于有效的自我验证是必要的，以确保验证过程的准确性和可靠性。

  • 关键点（易漏细节）：
  • verifiable rewards仅在部分可验证的任务中有效。
  • checklist-based rewards将稀疏的通过/失败监督转换为更密集的部分信用信号。
  • item-level judgments的平均可以减少验证器噪声。
  • partial credit可能奖励不完整的响应。
  • self-验证容易导致奖励膨胀。
  • 需要显式稳定来防止自我验证的崩溃。
  • verifier质量和checklist质量都影响下游的RL结果。
  • 显式稳定对于有效的自我验证是必要的。

[2605.28561v1] Soft-SVeRL: Self-Verified Reinforcement Learning with Soft Rewards Computer Science > Computation and Language arXiv:2605.28561v1 (cs) [Submitted on 27 May 2026] Title: Soft-SVeRL: Self-Verified Reinforcement Learning with Soft Rewards Authors: Saurabh Dash , Pierre Clavier , John Dang , Matthias Galle , Marzieh Fadaee , Ahmet Üstün , Beyza Ermis View a PDF of the paper titled Soft-SVeRL: Self-Verified Reinforcement Learning with Soft Rewards, by Saurabh Dash and 6 other authors View PDF HTML (experimental) Abstract: Reinforcement Learning from Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has improved language models in domains such as mathematics and code, where correctness can be checked automatically. However, many important tasks are only partially verifiable: prompts contain multiple requirements, responses may satisfy some but not all of them, or no single reference answer might exist. We introduce Soft-RLVR, a framework for reinforcement learning from decomposed, learned verification signals. Soft-RLVR converts each prompt into a checklist of atomic requirements, scores candidate responses item by item with an LLM verifier, and trains on the resulting soft reward. Checklist-based rewards turn sparse pass/fail supervision into a denser partial-credit signal, but they also introduce a tradeoff: averaging item-level judgments can reduce verifier noise, while partial credit can reward incomplete responses. We formalize this tradeoff and identify conditions under which checklist-based verification gives a more reliable RL training signal than holistic verification. We further introduce Soft-SVeRL, a self-verifying variant of Soft-RLVR in which the policy also acts as the verifier. We show that self-verification is prone to reward inflation from overly permissive self-judgments, and that explicit stabilization is needed to prevent this collapse. In a controlled instruction-following setting with rule-based ground-truth evaluation, checklist-based Soft-RLVR improves IFEval by up to 11.1 points using only learned verifier rewards. Our experiments further show that verifier quality and checklist quality both affect downstream RL outcomes, and that explicit stabilization is essential for effective self-verification. 58. Gamma-World: Generative Multi-Agent World Modeling Beyond Two Players

  • 链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28816v1
  • 来源：arxiv
  • 摘要：World models for interactive video generation have largely focused on single-agent settings, where future observations are generated from a single control signal. However, many generated environments require multi-agent interaction: multiple players, robots, or embodied agents act simultaneously within a shared space. Scaling world models to such settings requires a principled multi-agent design: agents should remain independently controllable, permutation-symmetric, and support efficient inference while maintaining consistency across time and perspectives. In this paper, we present our generative multi-agent world model for interactive simulation. It introduces Simplex Rotary Agent Encoding, a parameter-free extension of 3D RoPE that represents agents as vertices of a regular simplex in rotary angle space. This gives each agent a distinct phase while making all agents permutation-equivalent, enabling scalable agent identity without learned per-slot identities or a fixed agent ordering. To avoid dense all-to-all attention across agents, we further propose Sparse Hub Attention, where learnable hub tokens mediate token interaction across agents, reducing cross-agent attention cost fr
  • 易漏细节梳理：

首先，为了确保智能体保持独立可控性，我们采用了Simplex Rotary Agent Encoding，通过将每个智能体映射到正则单纯形的顶点，确保每个智能体在置换等价的情况下具有独特的相位。其次，为了提高推理效率并支持实时模拟，我们设计了permutation-symmetric结构，这不仅保证了智能体之间的置换对称性，还使得智能体在不同时间点和视角下保持一致。此外，为了进一步降低跨智能体的注意力成本，我们引入了Sparse Hub Attention机制，通过可学习的hub令牌来中介令牌间的交互。因此，我们的模型能够在24 FPS下实现响应动作的生成，同时显著提高了视频保真度和动作可控性。此外，我们的模型能够从两到四个玩家进行泛化，无需额外训练，这进一步证明了其强大的适应性和灵活性。因此，这些设计不仅提高了模型的性能，还增强了其在多智能体系统中的应用潜力。

  • 关键点（易漏细节）：
  • agents should remain independently controllable
  • permutation-symmetric design is crucial
  • efficient inference supports real-time simulation
  • consistency across time and perspectives is required
  • Simplex Rotary Agent Encoding uses vertices of a regular simplex
  • each agent has a distinct phase while being permutation-equivalent
  • Sparse Hub Attention reduces cross-agent attention cost
  • learnable hub tokens mediate token interaction
  • action-responsive generation is enabled at 24 FPS
  • our model improves video fidelity and action controllability
  • it generalizes from two to four players without additional training

[2605.28816v1] Gamma-World: Generative Multi-Agent World Modeling Beyond Two Players Computer Science > Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition arXiv:2605.28816v1 (cs) [Submitted on 27 May 2026] Title: Gamma-World: Generative Multi-Agent World Modeling Beyond Two Players Authors: Fangfu Liu , Kai He , Tianchang Shen , Tianshi Cao , Sanja Fidler , Yueqi Duan , Jun Gao , Igor Gilitschenski , Zian Wang , Xuanchi Ren View a PDF of the paper titled Gamma-World: Generative Multi-Agent World Modeling Beyond Two Players, by Fangfu Liu and 9 other authors View PDF Abstract: World models for interactive video generation have largely focused on single-agent settings, where future observations are generated from a single control signal. However, many generated environments require multi-agent interaction: multiple players, robots, or embodied agents act simultaneously within a shared space. Scaling world models to such settings requires a principled multi-agent design: agents should remain independently controllable, permutation-symmetric, and support efficient inference while maintaining consistency across time and perspectives. In this paper, we present our generative multi-agent world model for interactive simulation. It introduces Simplex Rotary Agent Encoding, a parameter-free extension of 3D RoPE that represents agents as vertices of a regular simplex in rotary angle space. This gives each agent a distinct phase while making all agents permutation-equivalent, enabling scalable agent identity without learned per-slot identities or a fixed agent ordering. To avoid dense all-to-all attention across agents, we further propose Sparse Hub Attention, where learnable hub tokens mediate token interaction across agents, reducing cross-agent attention cost from quadratic to linear in the number of agents. For real-time rollout, we distill a full-context diffusion teacher into a causal student that generates temporal blocks sequentially with KV caching, enabling action-responsive generation at 24 FPS. Experiments in multiplayer virtual environments show that our model improves video fidelity, action controllability, and inter-agent consistency over slot-based and dense-attention baselines, while generalizing from two to four players without additional training. 59. Entropic backreaction from cosmic structure formation: a thermodynamic approach to the late-time cosmological tensions

  • 链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28797v1
  • 来源：arxiv
  • 摘要：High-precision cosmological observations have revealed persistent tensions within the standard $Λ$CDM paradigm, most notably the discrepancy in the Hubble constant and the lower than predicted amplitude of late-time matter clustering quantified by $S_8$. We propose a unified thermodynamic framework in which entropic backreaction generated during cosmic structure formation modifies both the background expansion history and the growth of matter perturbations. As gravitational instability drives the growth of cosmic structures, the configuration entropy associated with the matter distribution decreases through the nonlinear redistribution of gravitational binding energy. The resulting entropic energy density contributes a late-time backreaction that enhances the cosmic expansion rate without altering early-Universe physics or the CMB sound horizon. Simultaneously, the same irreversible entropy dissipation process induces a dissipative correction within the cosmic velocity flow, suppressing the efficiency of coherent gravitational clustering at late times. The framework operates entirely within standard General Relativity: the Einstein field equations, Poisson equation, and gravitation
  • 易漏细节梳理：

entropic backreaction 是一种热力学驱动机制，它可能会改变背景膨胀历史和物质扰动的增长，同时提供了一个理论上保守且可观察检验的机制。非线性重分布引力束缚能导致物质分布的配置熵减少，而entropic backreaction 不会改变早期宇宙物理或CMB声速视界。此外，entropic backreaction 可以缓解晚期宇宙的紧张，不可逆的熵耗散过程会抑制晚期的有序引力聚集体效率。尽管如此，Einstein场方程、泊松方程和引力耦合保持不变，没有引入新的传播自由度或第五种力。因此，entropic backreaction 机制完全基于标准广义相对论，为理解宇宙膨胀和物质分布提供了新的视角。

  • 关键点（易漏细节）：
  • entropic backreaction 可能会改变背景膨胀历史和物质扰动的增长。
  • 非线性重分布引力束缚能导致物质分布的配置熵减少。
  • entropic backreaction 不会改变早期宇宙物理或 CMB 声速视界。
  • entropic backreaction 提供了一个热力学驱动的、理论上保守的、可观察检验的机制。
  • entropic backreaction 可能同时缓解了主要的晚期宇宙紧张。

  • entropic backreaction 机制完全基于标准广义相对论。

[2605.28797v1] Entropic backreaction from cosmic structure formation: a thermodynamic approach to the late-time cosmological tensions Astrophysics > Cosmology and Nongalactic Astrophysics arXiv:2605.28797v1 (astro-ph) [Submitted on 27 May 2026] Title: Entropic backreaction from cosmic structure formation: a thermodynamic approach to the late-time cosmological tensions Authors: Biswajit Pandey View a PDF of the paper titled Entropic backreaction from cosmic structure formation: a thermodynamic approach to the late-time cosmological tensions, by Biswajit Pandey View PDF HTML (experimental) Abstract: High-precision cosmological observations have revealed persistent tensions within the standard $\Lambda$CDM paradigm, most notably the discrepancy in the Hubble constant and the lower than predicted amplitude of late-time matter clustering quantified by $S_8$. We propose a unified thermodynamic framework in which entropic backreaction generated during cosmic structure formation modifies both the background expansion history and the growth of matter perturbations. As gravitational instability drives the growth of cosmic structures, the configuration entropy associated with the matter distribution decreases through the nonlinear redistribution of gravitational binding energy. The resulting entropic energy density contributes a late-time backreaction that enhances the cosmic expansion rate without altering early-Universe physics or the CMB sound horizon. Simultaneously, the same irreversible entropy dissipation process induces a dissipative correction within the cosmic velocity flow, suppressing the efficiency of coherent gravitational clustering at late times. The framework operates entirely within standard General Relativity: the Einstein field equations, Poisson equation, and gravitational coupling remain unmodified, and no new propagating degrees of freedom or fifth forces are introduced. Entropic backreaction therefore provides a thermodynamically motivated, theoretically conservative, and observationally testable mechanism that may simultaneously alleviate the major late-time cosmological tensions. 60. Isotropic fabrication of centimeter-scale, low propagation-loss periodically poled lithium niobate nanophotonic waveguides for efficient second harmonic generation

  • 链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28689v1
  • 来源：arxiv
  • 摘要：Periodically poled lithium niobate (PPLN) nanophotonic waveguides that simultaneously feature low propagation-loss and uniform periodic poling are essential for a wide range of applications ranging from classical nonlinear frequency-conversion to scalable integrated quantum technology. However, fabrication imperfections have frequently limited the propagation loss of fully domain-inverted PPLN nanophotonic waveguides to a few dB/cm, primarily due to anisotropic etching issue, thereby restricting the absolute conversion efficiency and scale of photonic integration. Here, we present a fabrication approach that overcomes this challenge, yielding a 1.2-cm-long PPLN nanophotonic waveguide with low propagation loss via femtosecond-laser photolithography-assisted chemo-mechanical etching (PLACE). By carrying out domain inversion on a planar thin-film prior to waveguide definition, electric-field distortion is minimized during poling, while isotropic etching of the waveguide is achieved by PLACE with an average surface roughness of only 0.34 nm, resulting in uniform poling of duty cycle of 50% and a record-low propagation loss of 0.042 dB/cm in the telecom band. Under continuous-wave pumpi
  • 易漏细节梳理：

首先，通过使用飞秒激光光刻辅助化学机械蚀刻（femtosecond-laser photolithography-assisted chemo-mechanical etching），实现了各向同性蚀刻（isotropic etching），进而解决了各向异性蚀刻问题（anisotropic etching issue），从而限制了传播损耗（limits propagation loss）。其次，通过PLACE（PLAce）技术，实现了均匀的极化（uniform poling），其平均周期为单周期（single-period），且均匀周期的占空比为50%，这有助于最小化电场失真（electric-field distortion）并保持低传播损耗（low propagation loss）至0.042 dB/cm。此外，通过维持0.34 nm的平均表面粗糙度，进一步确保了高规范化的二次谐波生成（high normalized quasi-phase-matched SHG conversion efficiency），最终实现了64%的绝对转换效率（absolute conversion efficiency），这标志着单周期PPLN纳米光子波导的最新水平（state of the art for single-period PPLN nanophotonic waveguides）。因此，这种纳米光子波导的制造（cm-long PPLN nanophotonic waveguide is fabricated）不仅展示了高效率的二次谐波生成，还为单周期PPLN纳米光子波导设定了新的标准。

  • 关键点（易漏细节）：
  • anisotropic etching issue limits propagation loss
  • isotropic etching is achieved by PLACE
  • average surface roughness of 0.34 nm is maintained
  • uniform poling of duty cycle 50% is obtained
  • low propagation loss of 0.042 dB/cm is achieved
  • electric-field distortion is minimized during poling
  • femtosecond-laser photolithography-assisted chemo-mechanical etching is used
  • cm-long PPLN nanophotonic waveguide is fabricated
  • high normalized quasi-phase-matched SHG conversion efficiency is demonstrated
  • absolute conversion efficiency of 64% is achieved
  • state of the art for single-period PPLN nanophotonic waveguides is set

[2605.28689v1] Isotropic fabrication of centimeter-scale, low propagation-loss periodically poled lithium niobate nanophotonic waveguides for efficient second harmonic generation Physics > Optics arXiv:2605.28689v1 (physics) [Submitted on 27 May 2026] Title: Isotropic fabrication of centimeter-scale, low propagation-loss periodically poled lithium niobate nanophotonic waveguides for efficient second harmonic generation Authors: Guanghui Zhao , Yixuan Yang , Renhong Gao , Jintian Lin , Ya Cheng View a PDF of the paper titled Isotropic fabrication of centimeter-scale, low propagation-loss periodically poled lithium niobate nanophotonic waveguides for efficient second harmonic generation, by Guanghui Zhao and 4 other authors View PDF Abstract: Periodically poled lithium niobate (PPLN) nanophotonic waveguides that simultaneously feature low propagation-loss and uniform periodic poling are essential for a wide range of applications ranging from classical nonlinear frequency-conversion to scalable integrated quantum technology. However, fabrication imperfections have frequently limited the propagation loss of fully domain-inverted PPLN nanophotonic waveguides to a few dB/cm, primarily due to anisotropic etching issue, thereby restricting the absolute conversion efficiency and scale of photonic integration. Here, we present a fabrication approach that overcomes this challenge, yielding a 1.2-cm-long PPLN nanophotonic waveguide with low propagation loss via femtosecond-laser photolithography-assisted chemo-mechanical etching (PLACE). 61. Satisfiability Solving with LLMs: A Matched-Pair Evaluation of Reasoning Capability

  • 链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28602v1
  • 来源：arxiv
  • 摘要：Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for tasks that implicitly reduce to Boolean satisfiability (SAT), yet their reasoning ability on SAT remains unclear. We present a systematic study of LLMs on 2-SAT and 3-SAT, together with two canonical reductions, Vertex Cover and discrete 3D packing, to probe representation-invariant reasoning. We first evaluate models using conventional metrics, including accuracy, precision, recall, and F1, as well as the SAT phase-transition setting. We find that these metrics can be misleading: many models obtain high scores by over-predicting satisfiable formulas, fail to reproduce the classical easy-hard-easy signature around the 3-SAT threshold, and degrade sharply as the number of variables grows. To address this problem, we introduce a paired-formula protocol based on minimally different satisfiable and unsatisfiable instances, together with Accurate Differentiation Rate (ADR), which requires both members of each pair to be classified correctly. ADR separates reasoning-oriented models from heuristic ones and correlates with witness validity. Beyond CNF, we test cross-representation consistency by converting CNF to Vertex Cover and 3-S
  • 易漏细节梳理：

首先，过预测可满足公式会导致高分数但推理能力较差，这揭示了模型在处理复杂问题时的局限性。其次，经典的易-难-易签名特征在3-SAT阈值附近是SAT求解器的关键特性，这表明模型在接近阈值时的表现尤为显著。此外，随着变量数量的增加，模型的性能往往会下降，这是许多模型共有的现象。因此，为了更准确地区分推理导向型和启发式模型，可以采用配对公式协议。进一步地，准确区分率（ADR）与证词的有效性密切相关，这为评估模型的推理能力提供了新的视角。然而，传统的评估指标在评价LLM在SAT问题上的表现时可能会误导人，因为它们可能无法全面反映模型的推理能力。因此，通过将模型在CNF及其对应的图或包装实例上的决策进行交叉表示一致性分析，可以更好地理解模型的推理过程。此外，SAT作为保守的探针，可以用于评估LLM的推理能力，而配对评估与ADR相结合则能提供比传统指标更忠实的评估。最后，通过连接相关文献和使用litmaps工具，可以更好地理解这些研究的背景和相关工作。

  • 关键点（易漏细节）：
  • over-predicting satisfiable formulas can lead to high scores but poor reasoning ability.
  • the classical easy-hard-easy signature around the 3-SAT threshold is a key characteristic of SAT solvers.
  • degradation in performance as the number of variables increases is common among models.
  • paired-formula protocol helps distinguish between reasoning-oriented and heuristic models.
  • ADR (Accurate Differentiation Rate) correlates with the validity of witnesses.
  • conventional metrics can be misleading when evaluating LLMs on SAT problems.
  • model decisions on CNF and corresponding graph or packing instances show cross-representation consistency.
  • SAT is a conservative probe for evaluating LLM reasoning capabilities.
  • paired evaluation with ADR provides a more faithful assessment than conventional metrics.
  • connected papers and litmaps tools can help understand the context and related works.

[2605.28602v1] Satisfiability Solving with LLMs: A Matched-Pair Evaluation of Reasoning Capability Computer Science > Artificial Intelligence arXiv:2605.28602v1 (cs) [Submitted on 27 May 2026] Title: Satisfiability Solving with LLMs: A Matched-Pair Evaluation of Reasoning Capability Authors: Leizhen Zhang , Shuhan Chen , Sheng Chen View a PDF of the paper titled Satisfiability Solving with LLMs: A Matched-Pair Evaluation of Reasoning Capability, by Leizhen Zhang and 2 other authors View PDF HTML (experimental) Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for tasks that implicitly reduce to Boolean satisfiability (SAT), yet their reasoning ability on SAT remains unclear. We present a systematic study of LLMs on 2-SAT and 3-SAT, together with two canonical reductions, Vertex Cover and discrete 3D packing, to probe representation-invariant reasoning. We first evaluate models using conventional metrics, including accuracy, precision, recall, and F1, as well as the SAT phase-transition setting. We find that these metrics can be misleading: many models obtain high scores by over-predicting satisfiable formulas, fail to reproduce the classical easy-hard-easy signature around the 3-SAT threshold, and degrade sharply as the number of variables grows. To address this problem, we introduce a paired-formula protocol based on minimally different satisfiable and unsatisfiable instances, together with Accurate Differentiation Rate (ADR), which requires both members of each pair to be classified correctly. ADR separates reasoning-oriented models from heuristic ones and correlates with witness validity. Beyond CNF, we test cross-representation consistency by converting CNF to Vertex Cover and 3-SAT to discrete 3D packing. Model decisions on CNF and on the corresponding graph or packing instances agree for most models on more than 80 percent of instances, suggesting stable decision rules across representations. Overall, our results show that SAT is a conservative probe for LLM reasoning, and that paired evaluation with ADR provides a more faithful and representation-robust assessment than conventional metrics. 62. Synthesis and properties of bulk Mg$_3$WN$_4$ in a wurtzite-derived structure

  • 链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28799v1
  • 来源：arxiv
  • 摘要：Experimental synthesis of theoretically predicted materials with controlled elemental coordination environments can lead to realization of useful properties, such as facile ion transport or ferroelectric switching. Among such materials are new ternary nitrides in the Mg-W-N composition space, where several new stable and metastable compounds have been predicted and synthesized recently in bulk and film forms. Here, we report for the first time on the bulk synthesis of Mg$_3$WN$_4$ in a wurtzite-derived crystal structure via a solid state metathesis reaction. $In$ $situ$ synchrotron powder X-ray diffraction shows how the ion exchange proceeds from Li$_6$WN$_4$ + 3 MgCl$_2$ precursors to Mg$_3$WN$_4$ + 6 LiCl products, with the reaction starting slowly near 380 $^\circ$C and completing by 600 $^\circ$C, including the presence of a competing disordered rocksalt-derived phase (Mg,W)N above 440 $^\circ$C. The follow up $ex$ $situ$ powder synthesis at 400 $^\circ$C for 0.5 hour with 10% excess MgCl$_2$ reveals the cation-ordered nature of the wurtzite-derived Mg$_3$WN$_4$ structure with polar symmetry confirmed by second harmonic generation measurements. Optical absorption spectra, chemi
  • 易漏细节梳理：

solid state metathesis反应需要精确控制温度以完成反应，反应开始于约380°C附近，完成于600°C。在此过程中，存在一个竞争性的无序岩石盐衍生相（Mg,W）N，而Mg3WN4则具有阳离子有序的结构。值得注意的是，Mg3WN4容易形成缺陷，这在光学吸收谱、化学成分分析和电子显微镜成像中得到了证实。in situ测量有助于更好地理解相纯材料的合成，而ex situ合成则可以揭示材料的极性对称性。因此，精确控制温度对于确保Mg3WN4的形成和纯度至关重要。

  • 关键点（易漏细节）：
  • solid state metathesis reaction需要精确控制温度以完成反应。
  • 反应开始于380°C附近，完成于600°C。
  • 存在一个竞争性的无序岩石盐衍生相（Mg,W）N。
  • Mg3WN4具有阳离子有序的结构。
  • Mg3WN4容易形成缺陷。
  • in situ测量有助于理解相纯材料的合成。
  • ex situ合成可以揭示材料的极性对称性。

[2605.28799v1] Synthesis and properties of bulk Mg$_3$WN$_4$ in a wurtzite-derived structure Condensed Matter > Materials Science arXiv:2605.28799v1 (cond-mat) [Submitted on 27 May 2026] Title: Synthesis and properties of bulk Mg$_3$WN$_4$ in a wurtzite-derived structure Authors: Anna A. Berseneva , Christopher L. Rom , Layton Rudolph , Yunseung Kuk , P. Shiv Halasyamani , Rebecca W. Smaha , James R. Neilson , Andriy Zakutayev View a PDF of the paper titled Synthesis and properties of bulk Mg$_3$WN$_4$ in a wurtzite-derived structure, by Anna A. Berseneva and 7 other authors View PDF Abstract: Experimental synthesis of theoretically predicted materials with controlled elemental coordination environments can lead to realization of useful properties, such as facile ion transport or ferroelectric switching. Among such materials are new ternary nitrides in the Mg-W-N composition space, where several new stable and metastable compounds have been predicted and synthesized recently in bulk and film forms. Here, we report for the first time on the bulk synthesis of Mg$_3$WN$_4$ in a wurtzite-derived crystal structure via a solid state metathesis reaction. $In$ $situ$ synchrotron powder X-ray diffraction shows how the ion exchange proceeds from Li$_6$WN$_4$ + 3 MgCl$_2$ precursors to Mg$_3$WN$_4$ + 6 LiCl products, with the reaction starting slowly near 380 $^\circ$C and completing by 600 $^\circ$C, including the presence of a competing disordered rocksalt-derived phase (Mg,W)N above 440 $^\circ$C. The follow up $ex$ $situ$ powder synthesis at 400 $^\circ$C for 0.5 hour with 10% excess MgCl$_2$ reveals the cation-ordered nature of the wurtzite-derived Mg$_3$WN$_4$ structure with polar symmetry confirmed by second harmonic generation measurements. Optical absorption spectra, chemical composition analysis, and electron microscopy imaging suggests that bulk wurtzite Mg$_3$WN$_4$ is prone to defect formation. Overall, this study shows that selective $ex$ $situ$ synthesis of the phase pure ternary nitrides, informed by \textit{in situ} measurements, is possible by carefully controlling the thermal budget of the reaction, and paves a way towards property characterization of wurtzite Mg$_3$WN$_4$.
